# UI coverage (atlas v2)

Generated by v2/render_v2.py from capabilities.yaml.

> Rules: UI states come from a template per kind:
 > - list: list, loading, empty, error
 > - detail: detail, loading, error, not-found
 > - create: form, validation, pending, success, error
 > - update: edit, validation, pending, success, error
 > - delete: confirm, pending, error
 > - action: action-control, pending, error
 >
 > Extra needs:
 > - offline, if a mapped model is PowerSync-synced;
 > - variant:<group>, if roles span more than one of staff, learner, family or exec. A shared non-workspace screen counts as covering every group.

 > A state counts as existing if a regex matches a screen file on the path from route to call site. These are heuristics, so treat counts as ±.

### UI states / screens

- UI states needed, summed over code-derived capabilities: **2748**. Existing: **1234**. Missing: **1513**. On top of that, 808 PLANNED doc intents each need at least one screen.
- Screen files (route files plus Screen components across apps/packages): **330**.
  - On the path of a LIVE/PARTIAL capability (wired): **162**.
  - Showing real API data (path of a LIVE api/direct capability): **61**.
  - Coverage tiers: capability 184, capability-route(depth<=3) 78, ORPHAN-unrouted 64, ORPHAN-deep-in-closure 4
- Procedures: **259**. With UI reaching them: **151**. Without UI: **108**. LIVE: **86**. BACKEND-ONLY (no client site): **50**. DEAD-CALL: **0**.
